# IP Cross-Reference Integrity Sweep — 2026-05-20

Audit scope: read-only scan of `microservices/<µservice>/IP-*.md` and `microservices/<µservice>/ip/IP-*.md`.
Output path: `docs/architecture/ip-cross-reference-sweep-2026-05-20.md`.
Source IP files were not modified by this sweep.

## §1 Methodology

### §1.1 Citation Types

This sweep scores each IP slice against five citation classes required by documentation-rigor §3.2.5 and the user instruction for this audit.

| Citation type | Required evidence | Detection rule used in this sweep | Score contribution |
|---|---|---|---:|
| Journey support | At least one `jNN` or `jNNN` journey identifier, including journey-named IP paths. | Regex `j\d{2,3}` over path + body. | 1 |
| Related ADRs | At least five distinct ADR identifiers. | Unique `ADR-*` tokens over path + body; numeric and service-local ADR spellings count. | 1 |
| Cross-µservice handoff | At least one other µservice cited as a handoff/dependency. | Other µservice names in backticks, quotes, table cells, `microservices/<name>/` paths, or `service:`/`service=` fields. | 1 |
| Cedar policy | A Cedar policy, fragment, `.cedar` file, default-deny rule, or Cedar decision field. | Case-insensitive `cedar`, `.cedar`, `cedar_*`, `default-deny`, or `policy fragment`. | 1 |
| ADR-0263 audit-event class | Explicit audit event class citation from the ADR-0263 emission vocabulary. | `EVT-*`, `audit_event_class`, `audit-event class`, or `ADR-0263 event classes`. | 1 |

### §1.2 Scoring Rubric

| Score | Interpretation |
|---:|---|
| 5 | Fully cross-referenced: journey, ADR depth, handoff, Cedar, and ADR-0263 event class are all present. |
| 4 | Strong: one citation class missing; safe to remediate in normal doc-hardening flow. |
| 3 | Borderline: two citation classes missing; should be included in the next per-µservice IP-substance pass. |
| 2 | Substance-deficient: three citation classes missing; follow-up needed before treating the IP as execution-ready. |
| 1 | Severely deficient: only one citation class present. |
| 0 | Empty of required cross-references under this rubric. |

Substance-deficient threshold: score `< 3`.

### §1.3 Scan Boundary and Known Limits

- Included only root-level IP files and `ip/` subdirectory files under each direct child of `microservices/`, per the requested enumeration shape.
- Excluded nested `implementation-plans/`, `specs/`, and other non-requested directories even when they contain `IP-*.md` names.
- Cross-µservice handoff detection is citation-oriented, not semantic proof. It avoids bare noun matches for common service names and counts only explicit service-like citations.
- ADR-0263 event-class detection requires an event-class marker, not merely a generic observability paragraph.
- The report is an audit and corrective-delta inventory; it does not repair source IPs.

### §6.1 Corrective-Delta Template

For each deficient IP, add a small cross-reference block rather than rewriting the whole plan:

```markdown
## Cross-reference integrity
- Journey support: jNNN — <journey name and why this IP supports it>
- Related ADRs: ADR-0105, ADR-0131, ADR-0132, ADR-0145, ADR-0263
- Cross-µservice handoffs: <owning µservice> -> <other µservice> via <contract/event/saga>
- Cedar policy: <policy path or fragment id>; default-deny behavior and decision field
- ADR-0263 audit-event classes: EVT-...; mandatory fields include tenant_id, principal_id, trace_id, audit_id/evidence_hash as applicable
```

### §6.2 Follow-up Wave Shape

- Wave A: fix score 0-1 IPs first because they lack most of the execution safety context.
- Wave B: fix score 2 IPs next because they are officially substance-deficient by this sweep.
- Wave C: fix score 3 borderlines where ADR depth or ADR-0263 event classes are missing.
- Wave D: preserve score 5 exemplars by extracting local templates per high-volume µservice.
- Verification after remediation: rerun this exact sweep and require deficient count to monotonically decrease.

## §7 Verdict

Verdict: REVISE. The IP corpus is not cross-reference-complete under documentation-rigor §3.2.5 because `1130` of `2881` audited IPs score below 3/5.

The strongest systemic gaps are visible in the per-µservice table and full ledger below. The next corrective wave should patch citations, not source behavior: add journey IDs, ADR depth, explicit handoffs, Cedar policy references, and ADR-0263 event-class rows where missing.
